[1460] CLUSTER ID: FCT-C4411 | LOCATION: FCT
HEADLINE: Firm expands premium real estate portfolio with Chiroma’s court development    Byline  BAM Projects and Properties Ltd has announced the introduction of Chiroma’s Court, a premium residential development located in Gwarinpa, Abuja, as part of its ongoing commitment to delivering quality real estate investment opportunities across the Federal Capital Territory.  The development, which comprises elegantly designed four-bedroom terrace duplexes with Boys’ Quarters (BQ), further strengthens the company’s growing portfolio of residential and investment-focused projects within Abuja’s most sought-after locations.  Speaking on the project, the Managing Director of BAM Projects and Properties Ltd said the development reflects the company’s vision of creating communities that deliver both lifestyle value and long-term investment returns.  “At BAM, our focus goes beyond constructing buildings. We are committed to developing properties in strategic locations that provide comfort, security, and strong appreciation potential for homeowners and investors alike,” he said.  According to the company, Chiroma’s Court was strategically conceived to meet the growing demand for premium housing within Gwarinpa, one of Abuja’s most established residential districts.
